For minimization of G with sum fixed, projected gradient in log space: θ_i ← θ_i - η * (grad_i / a_i?) or multiplicative update a_i ← a_i / (1 + η grad_i/a_i)? Let's derive.
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a); m=1
    while m < 2*n-1: m*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, m)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
For gradient cross-correlation with weights w length L=2n-1 of a:

Thus h_i = conv(ar,w)[n-1+i] for i=0..n-1. This requires FFT length >= (n-1)+(2n-2)+1 =3n-2. Use 1<<(ceil log2). Compute via rfft. However w may be dense. Good.

```
def smooth_b(b, beta):
    max_b=np.max(b)
    exp=np.exp(beta*(b-max_b))  # avoid overflow; beta*b small
    sum_exp=exp.sum()
    value = max_b + np.log(sum_exp)/beta # soft max
    p=exp/sum_exp
```

L-BFGS implementation details:
```
def optimize_softmax_theta(theta0, n, deadline, beta_schedule=(20,100,400,1600)):
    theta = theta0.copy()
    def fjac(theta):
        t = np.clip(theta, -50, 50)
        z = np.exp(t - t.max())
        a = z / z.sum()
        b = conv_fft(a)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0) # for stability?
        mb = b.max()
        # If mb ==0 return
        e = np.exp(beta*(b-mb))
        se = e.sum()
        val = mb + np.log(se)/beta
        p = e/se
        # cross h
        h = cross_weights_a(p, a, n)
        avg = float(np.dot(p,b))
        g = 2.0 * a * (h - avg)
        # Optional: zero tiny
        # To keep sum exactly 1, the θ gradient formula already.
        return val, g
```

```
def step(a, K=200, eta=...):
   b=conv_fft(a); maxb=b.max(); idx=np.argsort(b)[-K:]
   p=np.zeros_like(b); p[idx]=1/K

   g=2*a*(h-avg)
   # subgradient of exact max; score=2n max. Since normalization fixed, we want reduce max.
   #.update θ: θ -= eta*g? But g is θ gradient for soft? For exact max subgradient in θ: a_i*(subgrad_a_i - avg_a), with subgrad_a=2h. We can use g_exact = a*(h-avg) (times2 irrelevant).
```

Pseudo:
```
def conv_conv(a, m): ...
def cross_w_a(w, a, M): ...

def optimize_theta(theta, n, deadline, maxcalls=200, beta=800):
    theta = theta.copy()
    call_count=0
    def fjac(theta):
       nonlocal call_count
       call_count +=1
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: raise TimeoutError
       t=np.clip(theta, -50, 50)
       z=np.exp(t - t.max())
       a=z/z.sum()
       b=conv(a); b=np.maximum(b, 0.0)
       mb=b.max()
       if mb<=0: return 0, np.zeros_like(a)
       eb=np.exp(beta*(b-mb))
       se=eb.sum()
       val=mb + np.log(se)/beta
       p=eb/se
       h=cross(p,a)
       avg=float(p.dot(b))
       g=2*a*(h-avg)
       # damp gradient norm to avoid large: scale to 1
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn>0: g = g * min(1.0, 0.1/gn)? But L-BFGS expects true gradient scaling; objective values ~1e-4, gradient ~? We can leave.
       return val,g
    res = minimize(fjac, theta, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':maxcalls,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-10})
    theta = res.x
    # normalize/cleanup
```

Initial one-sided generation:
```
def one_sided(n):
   c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1
   for k in range(1,n): c[k]=c[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
   return c
```

Cross h compute: Need p maybe sum normalized. `p` length L=2n-1. Use FFT M_cross. We can precompute `M_cross = 1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()`.
```
def cross_weights(p, a, M):
   ar = a[::-1].copy()
   fr = np.fft.rfft(ar, M)
   fp = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(fr*fp, M)
   return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```

Custom active descent implementation:
```
def active_descent(a, n, conv, deadline, max_iter=200, K=256):
    theta=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None))
    # normalize? theta as log a
    b=conv(bic); mb=b.max()
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time > deadline: break
       idx=np.argsort(b)[-K:]  # O(n log n) 5069 log, fine
       p=np.zeros_like(b); p[idx]=1/K
       h=cross(p,a)
       avg=p.dot(b)
       g=a*(h-avg)
       # line search: try eta in [1.0,0.5,0.25,...] scaled? g magnitude ~? theta update = -eta*g. eta maybe 10? Let's estimate g ~1e-5, eta 10 gives θ change 1e-4, small. Need eta maybe 1e4. We can normalize g norm and choose step in θ units.
       norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if norm==0: break
       # target θ step ~1e-2/norm? Try base = 0.01 / norm (so RMS θ change 0.01)
       base = 0.01 / max(norm,1e-12)
       accepted=False
       for eta in [base, base*2, base*0.5, base*0.25, base*0.1, base*0.05]:
          theta_new=theta - eta*g
          theta_new=np.clip(theta_new,-30,30)
          a_new=softmax(theta_new)
          b_new=conv; mb_new=b_new.max()
          if mb_new < mb - 1e-12:
             theta, a, b, mb=...
             accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # try smaller or break
          if base < 1e-8: break
          base *=0.2
    # return a
```

Block perturb search details:
```
def perturb_search(a, n, conv, deadline, rng, max_iters=2000):
   theta=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None))
   best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); # exact score known?
   for ...:
      if time>deadline: break
      # choose block
      width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//10)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      scale=float(rng.normal(0,0.1))
      theta[start:start+width]+=scale
      # occasionally global mirror or shift? theta = symmetric mix: theta = (1-0.3)*theta +0.3*theta[::-1]
      if rng.random()<0.1: theta = 0.5*(theta+theta[::-1])
      theta=np.clip(theta,-30,30)
      a_new=softmax(theta)
      b=conv; mb=b.max(); if mb < current_mb - 1e-12:
         try exact=evaluate; if finite and < best_score: update
      # also even if surrogate not better, with probability 0.01 exact? too much.
      # current_mb=mb if not update? For annealing, maybe accept worse occasionally. We'll just hill-climb.
```

```
def softmax(theta):
   t=np.clip(theta, -30, 30)
   z=np.exp(t-t.max())
   s=z.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return exp? else z/s
```

```
def consider(a):
   nonlocal best_list, best_score
   if a is None or len==0: return
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return
   a=a/s  # score invariant, but if max coefficient >1000? When normalize, max≤1; okay.
   # but if parent cap 1000 before normalize? If parent has huge values, clipping then normalize okay.
   try:
      score=float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
   except Exception:
      score=inf
   if np.isfinite(score) and score<best_score:
       best_score=score; best_list=[float(x) for x in a]
```

Let's implement `run_lbfgs(theta, n, conv, beta, maxfun, deadline)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(theta, ...):
   state={'count':0}
   def fjac(th):
      state['count']+=1
      if time.monotonic()>deadline:
         raise TimeoutError('lbfgs timeout')
      t=np.asarray(th,dtype=float)
      t=np.clip(t,-30,30)
      a=softmax(t)
      b=conv.b(a)
      # b may have tiny negative; set max part positive? 
      b0=np.maximum(b,0.0) # But for gradient p based on negative? Negative b shouldn't be in max; set negative to 0. However derivative wrt b of soft max if b negative and p=0 no issue. Use b_for= b0.copy()
      mb=float(b0.max())
      if mb <= 0: return SCALE*mb, np.zeros(n)
      exp_arg=beta*(b0-mb)
      # clip arg to [-745,0]
      exp_arg=np.clip(exp_arg, -745.0, 0.0)
      eb=np.exp(exp_arg)
      se=eb.sum()
      if not finite or se==0: ...
      p=eb/se
      h=conv.cross(p,a)
      avg=float(np.dot(p,b0))
      g=2.0*a*(h-avg)
      # Ensure finite
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.zeros(n)
      return SCALE*(mb + np.log(se)/beta), SCALE*g
   try:
       res=minimize(fjac, theta.copy(), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': max(20, maxfun//3), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
       theta=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
   except Exception:
       pass
   return np.clip(theta,-30,30)
```
Note: If we set b0=max(b,0), the objective is not exactly derivative of b for negative values but negligible. The gradient p uses b0. Good.

Convolution class:
```
class Convolver:
   def __init__(self,n):
      self.n=n; L=2*n-1
      m=1
      while m<L: m*=2
      self.mb=m
      Lc=3*n-2
      mc=1
      while mc<Lc: mc*=2
      self.mc=mc
   def b(self,a):
      fa=np.fft.rfft(a,self.mb)
      out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,self.mb)
      return out[:2*self.n-1]
   def cross(self,w,a):
      ar=a[::-1]
      fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,self.mc)
      fw=np.fft.rfft(w,self.mc)
      out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,self.mc)
      return out[self.n-1:self.n-1+self.n]
```

Now, family scan best could be one-sided; exact considering both raw and norm. Let's implement `consider_array` carefully:
```
def consider_array(arr, also_norm=True):
   nonlocal best_score, best_arr
   if arr is None: return
   arr=np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: return
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s < 0.01: return
   # raw candidate if max<=1000 (already)
   try:
      sc=float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
   except Exception:
      sc=np.inf
   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
      best_score=sc; best_arr=arr.copy()
   if also_norm:
```

```
   t=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64)
   t=np.clip(t, -30.0, 30.0)
   z=np.exp(t - np.max(t))
   s=z.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       z=np.ones(t.shape); s=z.sum()
   return z/s
...
```

Annealing algorithm:

```
def anneal_theta(theta, n, conv, deadline, rng, iters=500):
   theta=theta.copy(); current_a=softmax(theta); b=conv.b; curr_m=max
   best_theta=theta.copy(); best_m=curr_m; exact? We'll exact at end.
   temperature = 1e-3? θ changes.
   for it:
      # generate gaussian perturbation in theta of scale sigma, maybe decreasing
      sigma = base * (0.98**it) + 1e-3
      new_theta = theta + rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
      # maybe block? full gaussian.
      new_a=softmax(new_theta); new_b=conv.b; new_m=max
      delta = new_m - curr_m
      if delta<0 or rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temperature): accept
      if new_m<best_m: best_theta=...
   return best_theta
```

Now, exact evaluate of ones raw and normalized both score 2; two evals. okay.

Let's include parent initialization if `parent.size` maybe 2535. If parent size !=n and parent size>0, maybe use parent only if its size exactly n? For L-BFGS start, require size==n. For consider, if parent size can be any, but n chosen parent size if within range. If parent size >8192 and n=4096, consider parent[:4096]; okay.

Now, if `height_sequence_1` is unavailable, `parent` remains None, n=4096. Good.

Final code:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    ...
```

Let's now produce final. Ensure no analysis outside code.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


class _Conv:
    """FFT-based linear convolution helpers for a fixed length."""

    def __init__(self, n: int):
        self.n = int(n)
        m = 1
        need = 2 * self.n - 1
        while m < need:
            m <<= 1
        self.m_conv = m

        m2 = 1
        need2 = 3 * self.n - 2
        while m2 < need2:
            m2 <<= 1
        self.m_cross = m2

    def conv(self, a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, self.m_conv)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, self.m_conv)
        return out[: 2 * self.n - 1]

    def cross_p_a(self, p: np.ndarray, a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        # h_i = sum_{k=0}^{n-1} p_{i+k} a_k
        ar = a[::-1]
        fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, self.m_cross)
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, self.m_cross)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, self.m_cross)
        return out[self.n - 1 : self.n - 1 + self.n]


def _softmax_clipped(theta: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    t = np.clip(t, -30.0, 30.0)
    z = np.exp(t - float(np.max(t)))
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        z = np.ones_like(t)
        s = float(z.size)
    return z / s


def _one_sided(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        c[k] = c[k - 1] * float(2 * k - 1) / float(2 * k)
    return c


def _safe_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        hard_headroom = 25.0
        deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - hard_headroom)
    except Exception:
        deadline = t0 + 1.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        arr = _safe_arr(hs)
        if arr is not None:
            parent = arr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose a working length.  Prefer the supplied construction length when
    # it is in a reasonable dense/FFT-friendly range.
    if parent is not None and 64 <= parent.size <= 8192:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 4096
    n = max(64, int(n))

    conv = _Conv(n)
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None

    def consider(a, also_norm=True):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        if a is None:
            return
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            return
        a = _safe_arr(a)
        if a is None:
            return
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return

        if s >= 0.01:
            try:
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                sc = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = a.copy()

        if also_norm:
            an = a / s
            try:
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(an.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                sc = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = an.copy()

    def quick_score(a):
        a = _safe_arr(a)
        if a is None:
            return float("inf")
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return float("inf")
        a = a / s
        b = conv.conv(a)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(mb):
            return float("inf")
        return 2.0 * float(n) * mb

    # Baseline constructions.
    if parent is not None:
        consider(parent[:n] if parent.size != n else parent)

    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    consider(ones)

    c = _one_sided(n)
    consider(c)
    consider(c / float(c.sum()))
    safe_arr = _safe_arr(c / float(c.sum()))

    # A few cheap one-parameter families, evaluated first with FFT and then
    # exact-evaluated only for the best few shapes.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        tgrid = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        fam = []

        for p in (0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.10, 1.25, 1.50):
            try:
                a = np.power(c, p)
            except Exception:
                continue
            sc = quick_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                fam.append((sc, a))

        for lam in np.linspace(-0.25, 0.60, 17):
            try:
                a = c * np.exp(lam * tgrid)
            except Exception:
                continue
            sc = quick_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                fam.append((sc, a))

        fam.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        for _, a in fam[:3]:
            consider(a)

    # Warm-start and local refinement in log-softmax coordinates.
    starts = []

    def add_start(a):
        a = _safe_arr(a)
        if a is not None:
            a = a / float(a.sum())
            for b in starts:
                if np.linalg.norm(a - b) < 1e-12:
                    return
            starts.append(a)

    if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size == n:
        add_start(best_arr)
    add_start(c / float(c.sum()))
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        add_start(parent / float(parent.sum()))
    elif parent is not None:
        b = parent[:n]
        if float(b.sum()) > 0:
            add_start(b)

    def run_lbfgs(theta0, beta, maxfun):
        theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        theta0 = np.clip(theta0, -30.0, 30.0)

        state = {"last": theta0, "calls": 0}
        scale = 2000.0

        def fjac(theta):
            state["calls"] += 1
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                raise TimeoutError("lbfgs deadline")

            th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            th = np.clip(th, -30.0, 30.0)
            state["last"] = th.copy()

            a = _softmax_clipped(th)
            b = conv.conv(a)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            mb = float(np.max(b))
            if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb <= 0.0:
                return scale * mb, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

            ex = beta * (b - mb)
            ex = np.clip(ex, -745.0, 0.0)
            eb = np.exp(ex)
            se = float(np.sum(eb))
            if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
                return scale * mb, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

            p = eb / se
            h = conv.cross_p_a(p, a)
            avg = float(np.dot(p, b))
            g = 2.0 * a * (h - avg)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

            val = mb + float(np.log(se)) / float(beta)
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                val = 1e6
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

            return scale * val, scale * g

        try:
            if _scipy_minimize is None:
                return state["last"]
            maxfun = int(max(50, min(maxfun, 600)))
            maxiter = int(max(20, maxfun // 2))
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                fjac,
                theta0.copy(),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            # Prefer the last feasible point; this survives a late timeout.
            return state["last"]
        except Exception:
            return state["last"]

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0 and len(starts) > 0:
        maxfun_base = 300 if n <= 4096 else 180
        if float(budget_s) > 800:
            maxfun_base = int(maxfun_base * 1.5)

        for a0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
                break
            theta = np.log(np.clip(a0, 1e-300, None) + 1e-300)
            for beta in (800.0, 3000.0):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
                    break
                remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
                mf = int(max(80, min(maxfun_base, int(remaining * 10))))
                if mf < 80:
                    break
                theta = run_lbfgs(theta, beta, mf)
                a_new = _softmax_clipped(theta)
                consider(a_new)

    # Short simulated-annealing polish in log-space.  This is bounded by time
    # and only keeps the best FFT-surrogate point per run.
    def anneal(theta0, iters=350):
        theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        theta = np.clip(theta, -30.0, 30.0)
        a = _softmax_clipped(theta)
        b = conv.conv(a)
        cur_m = float(np.max(np.maximum(b, 0.0)))
        best_theta = theta.copy()
        best_m = cur_m
        temp = 4e-5
        sigma = 0.09

        for it in range(iters):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            th_new = theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            th_new = np.clip(th_new, -30.0, 30.0)
            a_new = _softmax_clipped(th_new)
            b_new = conv.conv(a_new)
            m_new = float(np.max(np.maximum(b_new, 0.0)))

            if not np.isfinite(m_new):
                continue

            delta = m_new - cur_m
            if delta < 0.0 or (np.isfinite(temp) and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / temp)):
                theta, a, cur_m = th_new, a_new, m_new

            if m_new < best_m:
                best_m = m_new
                best_theta = theta.copy()

            sigma *= 0.995
            temp *= 0.995
            if temp < 1e-12:
                break

        return best_theta

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        for a0 in starts[:2]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                break
            theta0 = np.log(np.clip(a0, 1e-300, None) + 1e-300)
            theta_new = anneal(theta0, iters=300)
            consider(_softmax_clipped(theta_new))

    # Try a longer one-sided baseline if time remains.  Larger length makes
    # the known asymptotic construction slightly closer to its limit.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        L2 = 4096
        if n < L2:
            c2 = _one_sided(L2)
            consider(c2)
            consider(c2 / float(c2.sum()))

    # Uniform scaling can change only floating-point evaluator behavior.
    # Test a few valid scales of the best shape.
    if best_arr is not None:
        sax = _safe_arr(best_arr)
        if sax is not None:
            max_ax = float(np.max(sax))
            for sc in (0.1, 1.0, 10.0, 100.0):
                if max_ax * sc > 1000.0:
                    continue
                if float(sax.sum()) * sc < 0.01:
                    continue
                consider(sax * sc, also_norm=False)

    if best_arr is None or best_score == float("inf"):
        best_arr = safe_arr if safe_arr is not None else np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        if best_arr.size == 0:
            best_arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    if (
        len(out) == 0
        or any((not np.isfinite(x)) or x < 0.0 or x > 1000.0 for x in out)
    ):
        out = [1.0]

    return out
```
